- **scylladb-docs**: Official ScyllaDB documentation, answered with links to the exact pages. Use for any ScyllaDB question, including CQL and data modeling, Alternator (DynamoDB-compatible API), shard-aware drivers, cluster operations, ScyllaDB Cloud, Manager and Operator, or migration from Cassandra or DynamoDB. - **scylladb-kubernetes**: Guide users deploying and operating ScyllaDB on Kubernetes via the official ScyllaDB Operator. Use this skill when a user is setting up a ScyllaCluster, configuring node storage/CPU pinning for ScyllaDB pods, installing the Operator, or troubleshooting a Kubernetes-based ScyllaDB deployment. Managed ScyllaDB Cloud clusters go to scylladb-cloud-setup. Triggers on "ScyllaDB Operator", "ScyllaCluster", "scylla-operator", "ScyllaDB on Kubernetes", "ScyllaDB Helm chart", "NodeConfig", "ScyllaDB EKS", "ScyllaDB GKE", "ScyllaDB OKE", "ScyllaDB kubernetes deployment", "exposeOptions", "ScyllaCluster… ([scylladb/agent-skills](https://github.com/scylladb/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scylladb-kubernetes) · ★9)
- **scylladb-principal-engineer**: Principal/Senior-level ScyllaDB playbook for shard-aware data modeling, consistency, compaction, topology, performance tuning, and operating high-throughput distributed wide-column systems. Use when: designing ScyllaDB schemas, tuning low-latency clusters, reviewing topology and repair posture, or operating ScyllaDB in production. - **scylladb-vector-search**: Guides users through implementing and optimizing ScyllaDB Vector Search and full-text search for semantic similarity, keyword search, RAG, and similar use cases. Use this skill when users need to store and query embeddings, build vector indexes (HNSW), run approximate nearest neighbour (ANN) queries, apply filtering (global/local secondary indexes), configure quantization, integrate with LLM frameworks (e.g. LangChain), create full-text indexes and BM25 queries, combine full-text and vector results (hybrid search), or run Vector Search locally with Docker for testing. - **scylladb-vector-search**: Guides ScyllaDB Cloud users through implementing and optimizing Vector Search for semantic similarity, RAG, and similar use cases. Use this skill when users need to store and query embeddings, build vector indexes (HNSW), run approximate nearest neighbour (ANN) queries, apply filtering (global/local secondary indexes), configure quantization, or integrate with LLM frameworks (e.g. LangChain). Also use when users mention "vector", "embeddings", "similarity search", "ANN", "nearest neighbor", "RAG", "semantic search", or "recommendation system" in the context of ScyllaDB. - **sd**: Find-and-replace text using `sd` — an intuitive `sed` replacement with normal regex syntax and `$1` capture groups. Use this skill for search-and-replace across files or streams: renaming a symbol, rewriting a string, stripping/normalising text in a pipe, or applying a regex substitution. Prefer sd over sed when the sed syntax is fiddly. Pairs with fd/rg to scope which files get edited. Edits files in place (no backup) — preview first with `-p`. Install: winget chmln.sd. - **sd-create-booking-page**: Create, list, inspect, and embed booking pages from the SimplerDevelopment portal. Two flows — (A) embed an existing booking page into a CMS page, deck, or email via the `booking` block (or `booking-menu` for all-services), or (B) author a new booking page via `booking_pages_create` + `booking_pages_update`, which mints an approval URL whose approval flips `active=true` so `/book/[slug]` starts accepting reservations. Returns the public `/book/[slug]` URL. - **sd-jwt-examples-nested**: Use when implementing nested SD-JWT data handling. Covers: flat, structured, and recursive disclosure examples for complex claim sets. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-examples-nested))
- **sd-jwt-format**: Use when working with SD-JWT data formats. Covers: SD-JWT and SD-JWT+KB structure, disclosures, salt/hash mechanisms, and decoy digests. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-format))
- **sd-jwt-intro**: Use when understanding SD-JWT (Selective Disclosure JWT) concepts and terminology. Covers: introduction, use cases, and key definitions. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-intro))
- **sd-jwt-quickstart**: Use when implementing SD-JWT end-to-end quickly. Covers: issuer construction, holder presentation, verifier validation, SD-JWT VC metadata handling, OpenID4VP integration, and common pitfalls. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-quickstart))
- **sd-jwt-security**: Use when reviewing SD-JWT security considerations. Covers: threat model, hash collision, claim name collisions, key binding enforcement, and privacy considerations for selective disclosure. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-security))
- **sd-jwt-vc-intro**: Use when understanding the SD-JWT VC credential format. Covers: introduction, terminology, and the vct (Verifiable Credential Type) claim. ([sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge](https://github.com/sourcelabbg/eudi-knowledge/tree/HEAD/plugins/eudi/skills/sd-jwt-vc-intro))
- **sd-jwt-vc-metadata**: Use when configuring SD-JWT VC issuer or type metadata. Covers: header parameters, registered claims, issuer-signed JWT, issuer metadata, and type metadata resolution. - **sd-jwt-vc-presentation-security**: Use when presenting or verifying SD-JWT VCs. Covers: presentation in OpenID4VP, JWT claims, security considerations, and privacy considerations. - **sd-jwt-verification**: Use when implementing SD-JWT verification or JWS serialization. Covers: SD-JWT verification, holder processing, verifier verification, JWS JSON serialization formats. - **sd-parallel**: 当功能计划中有多个独立任务需要并行开发、用户说"并行执行""同时做""分发任务"时使用。通过子代理分发实现并行执行，并对每个子代理的输出做合规审查和质量审查。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/parallel) · ★1)
- **sd-pattern-discipline**: Pattern discovery and adherence rules for specwright. New code must read as if written by the team that wrote the surrounding code. Applied by `sd-spec-architect` (authoring `Pattern refs`), `sd-implementer` (following them), and `sd-reviewer` (verifying conformance). ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-pattern-discipline) · ★10)
- **sd-payroll**: Compute South Dakota 2026 employer reemployment assistance contributions, taxable wage base, new and construction rates, investment and administrative fees, Form 21 quarterly filing and no state income-tax withholding. Trigger on South Dakota payroll, SUI, RA tax, UI rate notice and quarterly wages. ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sd-payroll) · ★498)
- **sd-pipeline-sufficiency**: Use when asked "do we have enough pipeline to hit target", "pipeline sufficiency for [director]", "can we make target next quarter", "U2–U5 pipeline coverage", "pipeline check for [director name]", or "pipeline report for [director]". Covers sales director teams with AE-level drill-down, UCO momentum scoring, U1/U2 pipeline accelerators, U5 go-live management, and full FY (current + next 3 quarters) verdict. ([sarah-hord-db/vibe](https://github.com/sarah-hord-db/vibe/tree/HEAD/plugins/fe-manager/skills/sd-pipeline-sufficiency))
- **sd-plan**: Interview the user into a work item under docs/work, review the plan, and open its branch. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-plan) · ★1)
- **sd-plan-tests**: The test-planning craft skill (software-delivery SDLC step 5). Run it AFTER front-authorization has frozen an atom's ACs and BEFORE implementation, to derive a per-AC test plan — happy / edge / negative cases plus the change-attributable baseline case for each pre-change checkpoint — mapped to the acceptance-contract checkpoints, and emit a test-plan note keyed to the AC-IDs. ADVISORY craft: it produces a plan, it never gates, approves, or merges; the merge floor (the adopter's branch protection + CI checks — see the plugin's docs/merge-floor.md) is the merge authority. ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-plan-tests))
- **sd-planning**: Generate a feature plan through iterative problem breakdown, codebase research, and clarifying questions before handing off to tlc-spec-driven and requirements-table generation. Use when user says 'plan this feature step by step', 'help me plan this properly', 'generate a plan for this problem', or 'walk through planning this feature'. ([Ronnasayd/aipp](https://github.com/Ronnasayd/aipp/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-planning) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sd-port-fidelity**: Cross-project port fidelity policy for specwright. A port reproduces a donor implementation inside a host repo; the donor's form is the specification, and every departure from it is a citable decision rather than a preference. Authored into port specs by `sd-spec-architect` and enforced against the changeset by `sd-reviewer`. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-port-fidelity) · ★10)
- **sd-prompt-forge**: Create production-ready Stable Diffusion prompts for stylized character art and multi-character scenes. Use when working with SDXL, Illustrious XL, Pony, Forge or A1111, LoRA stacks, negative prompts, regional prompting, Forge Couple, ControlNet, IP-Adapter, or character-consistency workflows. ([Edemckb/sd-prompt-forge](https://github.com/Edemckb/sd-prompt-forge/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-prompt-forge))
- **sd-prompter**: Configure Stable Diffusion / Flux inference for a given prompt, style, and quality bar. Use when you need help with sd prompter. ([anubhavg-icpl/vibe](https://github.com/anubhavg-icpl/vibe/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-prompter) · ★13)
- **sd-proposal**: Use when the user wants to develop an evidence-backed, decision-ready proposal with transparent alternatives, investment, risks, success criteria, and an explicit ask. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-proposal) · ★1)
- **sd-prose-lint**: Use when prose written on the user's behalf — skill bodies, docs, README text, commit or PR text, release notes, outbound drafts — needs a prose lint, style lint, or AI-tell check before it is committed or sent; runs the deterministic Vale gate where it exists and assigns every finding a disposition, degrading gracefully where Vale is absent. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/contrib/sd-prose-lint) · ★1)

- **sd-recon-engine**: 【套装内部技能 · 全流程用户通常无需直接调用；直接说需求给总控 short-drama-director 即可，总控会自动路由到本技能】【漫剧老李 V7.5.0-SLIM 套装 · 子技能 · 〇·1 素材来源锁 · 对标采风】素材采风与对标情报引擎（轻量版）：用户说「我要对标某个爆款」时，把该剧的公开信息从全网（含外网）找回来，按固定格式交付一份《对标情报卡》。核心能力——短剧信源分级清单（平台官方榜 / 数据指数站 / 行业媒体 / 社媒讨论 / 外网指数）、采风四步（定目标→定信源→检索→落卡）、对标情报卡十三项字段模板（含可迁移点与不可迁移点）、六条证据纪律（不编造 / 时点必标 / 来源必标 / 一手与转述分开 / 事实与推断分开 / 独立信源数才是热度参考）。触发词：对标爆款、扒一下、采风、找资料、收集信息、热点、爆款分析、参考这部、题材调研、品类调研、市场调研。本技能为套装成员，完整全流程从总控 short-drama-director 进入；〇·1 素材来源锁由总控选定。 ([lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill](https://github.com/lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill/tree/HEAD/sd-recon-engine) · ★2.2k)

- **sd-regression**: Catch unintended visual changes in the SMART Disburse onboarding — from colleagues' commits (shared checkbox, scripts, styles) or from our own work. Lists which commits reach SD pages, then screenshots every SD page and dialog before/after at 375/768/1280 and shows only what changed. Use at the start of the day, before taking in origin/main, before pushing, or when asked "what did &lt;colleague&gt; change that affects SD". ([vasileschidu/test-sx](https://github.com/vasileschidu/test-sx/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-regression) · ★1)
- **sd-replan-loop**: Sanctioned mid-execution re-plan protocol for specwright. Lets a workflow adapt when execution (or the batch review) reveals that `02-tasks.md` is wrong, **without** violating spec immutability or skipping a gate. Referenced at runtime by `/sd:feature` and `/sd:refactor` - the only two workflows that produce a `01-plan.md` + `02-tasks.md` pair to re-plan. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-replan-loop) · ★10)
- **sd-research**: Use when the user asks for deep, multi-source research on a question, topic, or writing piece and wants a verified, source-graded written brief or research notes rather than a quick answer. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-research) · ★1)
- **sd-research-repo**: Use when writing, rendering, reviewing, or publishing a document in a research repo that follows the shared research-repo standard — the numbered layout, provenance blocks, Status sections, and the outward mirrors. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-research-repo) · ★1)
- **sd-retro-lessons**: Lesson-extraction discipline for specwright. Turns retro prose into privacy-safe, reusable one-line rules that transfer to a codebase the author has never seen. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-retro-lessons) · ★10)
- **sd-review**: Test due system design concepts through bounded cold recall and changed examples. ([denelbjohn18/system-design](https://github.com/denelbjohn18/system-design/tree/HEAD/skills/review) · ★2)
- **sd-review**: Review the exact current diff with local providers and dispose of the findings locally, never posting them. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review) · ★1)
- **sd-review**: 当用户希望审查代码时使用。即使用户只说"review 一下""检查代码""看看有没有问题""代码写完了""能不能合了""帮我看看""检查一下"也应触发。对照规格和任务计划检查 spec 合规性、代码质量，运行测试并做必要的 UI 验证。 ([wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills](https://github.com/wjgogogo/spec-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/spec/skills/review) · ★1)
- **sd-review**: The two-lens advisory review procedure the generic agent runs at the software-delivery review step (/foundry:sd-review, software-delivery review step). A PROCEDURE — it DISPATCHES the MERGED pr-reviewer general code lens (agents/pr-reviewer.md) ALWAYS, plus the security-reviewer security lens (agents/security-reviewer.md) WHEN the change touches auth/IAM, secrets/credentials, or supply-chain/dependencies, then collates the categorized findings for the operator. It does NOT re-implement review (the agents own the review craft). Advisory: it surfaces findings to the operator; it does NOT gate,… ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review))
- **sd-review-spec**: Review and correct a feature's spec documents (spec.md, design.md, tasks.md, plan.md, checks.md, requirements.md, context-map.md, ADRs) by verifying every claim against the real code, classifying gaps by severity, and applying fixes. Use when user says 'review this spec', 'audit the spec against the code', 'find gaps in the spec', 'check spec inconsistencies', 'find implicit requirements', 'validate design.md and tasks.md', or 'validate plan.md and checks.md'. ([Ronnasayd/aipp](https://github.com/Ronnasayd/aipp/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-review-spec) · ★3)

- **sd-run-flow**: Execute a SimplerDevelopment Workflow Designer agent flow — fetch the saved graph from the portal and actually run it, dispatching each node to its Claude Code persona subagent in graph order, pausing at human-in-the-loop nodes, and reporting live progress to the portal's Executions tab as it goes. Use when the user says 'run flow', 'execute the workflow', 'run the agent flow for project X', '/sd-run-flow', 'run the QA pipeline flow', or names a flow they built in the Workflow Designer canvas. Does not edit flows: it runs them and reports progress. ([SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment](https://github.com/SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sd-run-flow) · ★18)
- **sd-runtime-api**: Use when calling any sd.X identifier in a deck animation file, looking up the public surface of @whosejam/sd, choosing between Node subclasses, or picking a layout class from the layout() factory. ([whoseJam/sd](https://github.com/whoseJam/sd/tree/HEAD/packages/sd-agents/dist/claude/skills/sd-runtime-api) · ★13)

- **sd-sales-tax**: Compute South Dakota 2026 sales and use tax, local sales and municipal gross receipts tax. Trigger on South Dakota sales tax, services, remote sellers, marketplace providers, groceries, digital products, EPath, use tax and contractor or agricultural boundaries. Load us-sales-tax first. ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sd-sales-tax) · ★498)
- **sd-sales-tax**: Use this skill whenever asked about South Dakota sales tax, South Dakota use tax, South Dakota DOR filing, South Dakota Wayfair, South Dakota municipal gross receipts tax, or South Dakota sales tax compliance. Trigger on phrases like "South Dakota sales tax", "SD sales tax", "SDCL §10-45", "South Dakota DOR", "Wayfair", "South Dakota grocery tax", "South Dakota SST", "South Dakota no income tax", or any request involving South Dakota sales and use tax compliance. ALWAYS load us-sales-tax first for federal context. ([3ero/openaccountants](https://github.com/3ero/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sd-sales-tax))
- **sd-scheduler**: Use when the user asks to fill, extend, edit or check the Service Desk duty schedule (sd.wb.ru scheduler / Планировщик) — e.g. "проставь расписание", "заполни график до конца месяца", "проставь смены 10-10". - **sd-storyboard-engine**: 【套装内部技能 · 全流程用户通常无需直接调用；直接说需求给总控 short-drama-director 即可，总控会自动路由到本技能】【漫剧老李 V7.5.0-SLIM 套装 · 子技能】文戏/日常戏分镜专精引擎（短剧视感版）：短剧视感机位体系 + 镜头经济性 + 表演克制 + 段内衔接5维度与运镜5模式，将小说/剧本原文转化为极简文字分镜脚本；**并产出故事板图（★标准出图资产）：每段一张多面板图，按动作相位七态拆格（起势/过程/接触/顶点/结果/反应/出口），格数由动作复杂度决定而非机械九宫格，出口帧=下段入口帧，CLEAN_GRID 干净版供生图 + 可选技术标注版**。**★ 须感知 I 画风锁影像基底（真人/2D/3D/水墨）并按其适配脸部特写角度——3D/仿真人禁「特写×平视正面」组合、改带角度侧视（真人守 1/3 配额、2D 同场连用 ≤2）**。触发词：分镜、文字分镜、电影分镜、生成分镜、帮我分镜、导演分镜、镜头脚本、storyboard、镜头衔接、运镜衔接、故事板、故事版、分镜图、故事板图、面板链、四宫格故事板、四格故事板、六宫格故事板、九宫格故事板、2×2 故事板。本技能为套装成员，完整全流程从总控 short-drama-director 进入。 ([lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill](https://github.com/lixiaoxiao9888-create/manju-laoli-skill/tree/HEAD/sd-storyboard-engine) · ★2.2k)

Reads ONLY the test_recipe phase — the static_validation phase belongs to the separate sd… ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/tree/HEAD/skills/sd-test))

It IGNORES the test_recipe records (the separate sd-test step).
